  "channel_subchannels_anchored_here": [
    {
      "parent_channel": "Devotional Dependence and Rule",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "A dependent community turns toward a sovereign divine addressee through worship, service, obedience, and petition.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The devotional relation extends lexically from willing worship into ownership and coercive service, while lordship also carries the countervailing function of nurture.",
      "key": "C",
      "name": "Petition for Aid and Direction",
      "reading_type": "surface-primary",
      "scene_or_process": "Dependents seek assistance and gentle direction toward an upright path, with compassionate benefaction as the answering function.",
      "active_motifs": "requested aid and cooperation `ع و ن:B001/m01`; gentle guidance `ه د ي:B001/m01`; the traversable path `ص ر ط:B001/m01`; uprightness and rectitude `ق و م:B008/m01`; mercy enacted as benefaction `ر ح م:B001/m02`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا`, `ٱلصِّرَٰطَ`, and `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ه د ي`, `ص ر ط`, `ق و م`); 1:7 `صِرَٰطَ` (`ص ر ط`)",
      "synthesis": "Aid supplies the enabling force, guidance supplies the directing action, the path supplies its object, and straightness supplies the desired state. Mercy turns the request-response structure into beneficent rather than merely administrative rule.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:1",
        "1:3",
        "1:5",
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Benefaction, Welfare, and Praise",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Good is conferred through compassion and maintenance, stabilized as welfare, and answered by praise or recognition.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "Tenderness expands into practical provisioning, repair, guardianship, and structural support, while praise can function either as gratitude or as a claim imposed by a patron.",
      "key": "A",
      "name": "Compassionate Provision and Nurture",
      "reading_type": "mixed",
      "scene_or_process": "A caregiver feels tenderness, nurtures a dependent over time, guards its affairs, and supplies or repairs what sustains a good condition.",
      "active_motifs": "tenderness and compassion `ر ح م:B001/m01`; beneficent mercy `ر ح م:B001/m02`; conferred blessing `ن ع م:B001/m02`; good condition `ن ع م:B001/m01`; progressive nurture `ر ب ب:B002/m02`; guardianship and care `ق و م:B004/m01`; provision for dependents `غ ي ر:B001/m01`; repair of their equipment `غ ي ر:B001/m03`; practical aid `ع و ن:B001/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `غَيْرِ` and `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`غ ي ر`, `ن ع م`)",
      "synthesis": "Compassion is the motive, progressive nurture and guardianship are the continuing operations, and food, equipment, and assistance are the practical means. Blessing names the resulting condition of the cared-for dependent.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:1",
        "1:3",
        "1:2",
        "1:5",
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Benefaction, Welfare, and Praise",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Good is conferred through compassion and maintenance, stabilized as welfare, and answered by praise or recognition.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "Tenderness expands into practical provisioning, repair, guardianship, and structural support, while praise can function either as gratitude or as a claim imposed by a patron.",
      "key": "B",
      "name": "Patronage, Support, and Claimed Recognition",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "A patron supplies aid and the mainstay of another's life, then may present that support as a favor deserving praise.",
      "active_motifs": "aid and mutual support `ع و ن:B001/m01`; the mainstay of an affair `م ل ك:B005/m01`; structural and livelihood support `ق و م:B009/m01`; benefaction or favor `ن ع م:B001/m02`; blessing as a conferred good `ر ب ب:B016/m03`; seeking praise for a favor `ح م د:B005/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:2 `ٱلْحَمْدُ` and `رَبِّ` (`ح م د`, `ر ب ب`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` (`م ل ك`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)",
      "synthesis": "Aid, mainstay, and livelihood form one support mechanism. The social relation becomes patronage when the giver converts that material dependence into a demand for acknowledgment.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:2",
        "1:4",
        "1:5",
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Declared Identity and Public Presence",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Names, utterances, reputation, and bearing make identity or commitment socially legible.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The naming and address field reaches from divine invocation into assent, grammatical qualification, poetic exchange, and the ceremonial presence of a reputed captain.",
      "key": "C",
      "name": "Reputation and Poetic Exchange",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "Public speech circulates praise or blame until it becomes a person's audible reputation and social eminence.",
      "active_motifs": "good reputation or public voice `س م و:B008/m01`; social eminence `س م و:B001/m02`; exchanged praise or satire in verse `ه د ي:B011/m01`; the person of many praised qualities `ح م د:B003/m01`; soliciting praise for favors `ح م د:B005/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `ٱلْحَمْدُ` (`ح م د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`)",
      "synthesis": "Poetic exchange is the transmitting action, praise and satire supply opposite evaluations, and public voice is the resulting social state. Eminence describes the vertical effect of a reputation that others cannot easily match.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:1",
        "1:2",
        "1:6"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Declared Identity and Public Presence",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Names, utterances, reputation, and bearing make identity or commitment socially legible.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The naming and address field reaches from divine invocation into assent, grammatical qualification, poetic exchange, and the ceremonial presence of a reputed captain.",
      "key": "D",
      "name": "The Reputed Captain's Ceremonial Bearing",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "A maritime captain appears as a composed, publicly reputed leader whose station is marked by ceremonial scent.",
      "active_motifs": "captain or chief of sailors `ر ب ب:B017/m01`; perfume brazier `ع ب د:B012/m01`; calm and dignified bearing `ه د ي:B010/m01`; good public reputation `س م و:B008/m01`; an elevated visible figure `س م و:B002/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`)",
      "synthesis": "The captain supplies the leading role, calm bearing and visibility make that role perceptible, reputation carries it beyond the immediate crew, and the perfume brazier gives the scene a ceremonial object of status.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:1",
        "1:2",
        "1:5",
        "1:6"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Orientation, Passage, and Breakdown",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Movement is organized by a course, a guide, and a state of passage, then tested by deviation, engulfment, or loss of mobility.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The straight-road scene expands into landmarks, centerlines, and leading animals, while swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial supply a shape-motion analogy for passage into disappearance.",
      "key": "A",
      "name": "The Marked and Straight Route",
      "reading_type": "mixed",
      "scene_or_process": "A traveler receives guidance, reads landmarks, enters the center of a prepared road, and maintains a straight course that can also become an established manner of conduct.",
      "active_motifs": "gentle route guidance `ه د ي:B001/m01`; direction and manner `ه د ي:B002/m01`; road or straight path `ص ر ط:B001/m01`; straightness and rectitude `ق و م:B008/m01`; route landmark and boundary `ع ل م:B002/m02`; middle of the road or valley `م ل ك:B006/m01`; paved or trodden road `ع ب د:B005/m01`; established custom or habitual course `د ي ن:B005/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:2 `ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ` (`ع ل م`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` and `ٱلدِّينِ` (`م ل ك`, `د ي ن`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا ٱلصِّرَٰطَ ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ه د ي`, `ص ر ط`, `ق و م`); 1:7 `صِرَٰطَ` (`ص ر ط`)",
      "synthesis": "Guidance initiates movement, landmarks and boundaries make the route legible, a prepared surface and centerline stabilize traversal, and straightness describes its successful geometry. Direction and custom extend the same course structure from travel into sustained conduct.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:2",
        "1:4",
        "1:5",
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Orientation, Passage, and Breakdown",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Movement is organized by a course, a guide, and a state of passage, then tested by deviation, engulfment, or loss of mobility.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The straight-road scene expands into landmarks, centerlines, and leading animals, while swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial supply a shape-motion analogy for passage into disappearance.",
      "key": "B",
      "name": "The Visible Leader at the Front",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "A conspicuous leader takes the foremost position and draws a herd or procession behind it.",
      "active_motifs": "the foremost guide `ه د ي:B003/m01`; the leading animal followed by the group `م ل ك:B008/m01`; an elevated visible figure `س م و:B002/m01`; a gathered wild-cattle herd `ر ب ب:B014/m01`; a herd of wild donkeys `ع و ن:B006/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` (`م ل ك`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`)",
      "synthesis": "Visibility allows the leader to be tracked, the foremost position supplies direction, and the gathered animals supply the following participants. Leadership is thus rendered as a spatial relation before it becomes a social one.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:1",
        "1:2",
        "1:4",
        "1:5",
        "1:6"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Orientation, Passage, and Breakdown",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Movement is organized by a course, a guide, and a state of passage, then tested by deviation, engulfment, or loss of mobility.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The straight-road scene expands into landmarks, centerlines, and leading animals, while swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial supply a shape-motion analogy for passage into disappearance.",
      "key": "C",
      "name": "Engulfing and Penetrating Passage",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "An entity enters, cuts through, or is swallowed by another body or medium until it becomes hidden.",
      "active_motifs": "swallowing and disappearance through a passage `ص ر ط:B002/m01`; disappearance or burial within another medium `ض ل ل:B002/m01`; a stallion rising over and entering the herd `س م و:B003/m01`; a cutting, penetrating sword `ص ر ط:B003/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:6 and 1:7 `ٱلصِّرَٰطَ` / `صِرَٰطَ` (`ص ر ط`); 1:7 `ٱلضَّآلِّينَ` (`ض ل ل`)",
      "synthesis": "Swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial share a directional transition across a boundary. Their common outcome is loss of exterior visibility, whether the receiving medium is a throat, a body, or the earth.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:1",
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Orientation, Passage, and Breakdown",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Movement is organized by a course, a guide, and a state of passage, then tested by deviation, engulfment, or loss of mobility.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The straight-road scene expands into landmarks, centerlines, and leading animals, while swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial supply a shape-motion analogy for passage into disappearance.",
      "key": "D",
      "name": "Halted, Supported, and Recovered Movement",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "A moving body weakens or stops, relies on external support, and may recover enough strength to resume at a different pace.",
      "active_motifs": "an exhausted or broken-down mount `ع ب د:B011/m01`; an animal halted by fatigue `ق و م:B016/m02`; swaying supported walking `ه د ي:B008/m01`; balanced mature strength `ع و ن:B005/m01`; quick running `ع ب د:B009/m02`; brief delay before action `ع ب د:B009/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` and `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع ب د`, `ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` and `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ه د ي`, `ق و م`)",
      "synthesis": "Fatigue produces the halt, supporting companions temporarily replace lost balance, and restored strength changes the pace from delay to renewed movement. The scene distinguishes inability, assistance, and recovery as successive states.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:5",
        "1:6"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Orientation, Passage, and Breakdown",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Movement is organized by a course, a guide, and a state of passage, then tested by deviation, engulfment, or loss of mobility.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The straight-road scene expands into landmarks, centerlines, and leading animals, while swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial supply a shape-motion analogy for passage into disappearance.",
      "key": "E",
      "name": "Disorientation, Forgetting, and the Stray",
      "reading_type": "mixed",
      "scene_or_process": "A traveler, object, memory, or animal loses its intended relation to a route, owner, or knower.",
      "active_motifs": "deviation from guidance and aim `ض ل ل:B001/m01`; a possession whose location is lost `ض ل ل:B003/m01`; loss from memory `ض ل ل:B004/m01`; the ownerless stray animal `ض ل ل:B005/m01`; the dull or weak would-be guide `ه د ي:B009/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`); 1:7 `ٱلضَّآلِّينَ` (`ض ل ل`)",
      "synthesis": "The invariant is failed relational placement. A person loses the intended course, an object loses its owner or known location, knowledge leaves memory, and an animal leaves recognized custody.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Time, Measure, and Final Reckoning",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Time is made intelligible by bounded spans and celestial positions, by pace within a duration, and by a culminating event of judgment.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The day expands from ordinary daylight into zenith, lunar marking, personal pace, prolonged residence, and finally the event in which creation rises for sovereign accounting.",
      "key": "A",
      "name": "Daylight, Zenith, and Celestial Marking",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "A daylight span is located by the sun at zenith and by visible signs in the overhead sky.",
      "active_motifs": "bounded daylight `ي و م:B001/m01`; the sun standing at midday `ق و م:B017/m01`; the overhead sky `س م و:B004/m01`; a lunar station `ن ع م:B007/m04`; a distinguishing sign `ع ل م:B002/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ` (`ع ل م`); 1:4 `يَوْمِ` (`ي و م`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)",
      "synthesis": "Daylight supplies the bounded interval, zenith gives it an internal midpoint, and sky signs provide a larger celestial coordinate system. Time is read through the positions of bodies that appear to stand overhead.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:1",
        "1:2",
        "1:4",
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Time, Measure, and Final Reckoning",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Time is made intelligible by bounded spans and celestial positions, by pace within a duration, and by a culminating event of judgment.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The day expands from ordinary daylight into zenith, lunar marking, personal pace, prolonged residence, and finally the event in which creation rises for sovereign accounting.",
      "key": "B",
      "name": "Duration, Pace, and Praised Attainment",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "An actor inhabits a duration, varies between delay and haste, intensifies effort, and reaches a praiseworthy limit without losing composure.",
      "active_motifs": "an open duration of time `ي و م:B002/m01`; brief delay `ع ب د:B009/m01`; quick running `ع ب د:B009/m02`; increase or intensification `ن ع م:B010/m01`; the utmost praised goal `ح م د:B004/m01`; calm bearing `ه د ي:B010/m01`; continued residence `ر ب ب:B007/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:2 `ٱلْحَمْدُ` and `رَبِّ` (`ح م د`, `ر ب ب`); 1:4 `يَوْمِ` (`ي و م`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)",
      "synthesis": "Duration supplies the field, delay and running supply contrasting rates, and intensification directs those rates toward an upper limit. Continued residence and calm bearing keep attainment from collapsing into mere haste.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:2",
        "1:4",
        "1:5",
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Time, Measure, and Final Reckoning",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Time is made intelligible by bounded spans and celestial positions, by pace within a duration, and by a culminating event of judgment.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The day expands from ordinary daylight into zenith, lunar marking, personal pace, prolonged residence, and finally the event in which creation rises for sovereign accounting.",
      "key": "C",
      "name": "The Momentous Day of Rising and Account",
      "reading_type": "surface-primary",
      "scene_or_process": "A climactic event arrives, creation rises, and a sovereign conducts judgment, accounting, and recompense.",
      "active_motifs": "the momentous or calamitous day `ي و م:B003/m01`; resurrection and rising of creation `ق و م:B013/m01`; judgment, account, and recompense `د ي ن:B002/m01`; sovereign kingship `م ل ك:B003/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:4 `مَٰلِكِ يَوْمِ ٱلدِّينِ` (`م ل ك`, `ي و م`, `د ي ن`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`)",
      "synthesis": "The day is not only a duration but the event that triggers rising. Resurrection supplies the participants, sovereignty supplies the judge, and account and recompense supply the operation and outcome.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:4",
        "1:6"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Property, Measure, and Legitimate Transfer",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Goods and obligations move between parties through ownership, valuation, replacement, compensation, gift, or offering.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "Financial debt and equal coinage join a livestock market and its stray property, while compensation and substitution offer a regulated alternative to uncompensated loss.",
      "key": "A",
      "name": "Credit, Price, and Livestock Property",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "Livestock is owned, priced, sold on credit, and monitored for loss within an active market.",
      "active_motifs": "debt, loan, and deferred sale `د ي ن:B003/m01`; valuation and pricing `ق و م:B010/m01`; an equal-weight coin `ق و م:B015/m01`; a flourishing market `ق و م:B018/m01`; property and ownership `م ل ك:B002/m01`; livestock wealth `ن ع م:B005/m01`; an ownerless stray animal `ض ل ل:B005/m01`; a middle-aged animal fit for classification `ع و ن:B002/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` and `ٱلدِّينِ` (`م ل ك`, `د ي ن`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` and `ٱلضَّآلِّينَ` (`ن ع م`, `ض ل ل`)",
      "synthesis": "Ownership identifies the goods, age and kind make animals assessable, pricing and equal coin establish measure, and debt permits delayed settlement. The stray is the failure case in which market property loses recognized custody.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:4",
        "1:5",
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Property, Measure, and Legitimate Transfer",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Goods and obligations move between parties through ownership, valuation, replacement, compensation, gift, or offering.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "Financial debt and equal coinage join a livestock market and its stray property, while compensation and substitution offer a regulated alternative to uncompensated loss.",
      "key": "B",
      "name": "Replacement, Indemnity, and Redress",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "A lost or injured claim is answered by a substitute, transfer, or assessed compensation rather than remaining without redress.",
      "active_motifs": "blood money or indemnity `غ ي ر:B002/m01`; replacement of one thing by another `غ ي ر:B003/m02`; standing in another's place `ق و م:B007/m01`; blood lost without vengeance or compensation `ض ل ل:B003/m02`; judgment and recompense `د ي ن:B002/m01`; transfer of ownership `م ل ك:B002/m02`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:4 `مَٰلِكِ ... ٱلدِّينِ` (`م ل ك`, `د ي ن`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `غَيْرِ` and `ٱلضَّآلِّينَ` (`غ ي ر`, `ض ل ل`)",
      "synthesis": "Loss creates a vacant claim, substitution fills the vacant place, ownership transfer legalizes the replacement, and judgment fixes its compensatory value. Unredressed blood marks the opposite outcome.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:4",
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Property, Measure, and Legitimate Transfer",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Goods and obligations move between parties through ownership, valuation, replacement, compensation, gift, or offering.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "Financial debt and equal coinage join a livestock market and its stray property, while compensation and substitution offer a regulated alternative to uncompensated loss.",
      "key": "C",
      "name": "Reciprocal Gift and Beneficent Transfer",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "A gift moves toward a valued recipient as an expression of affection, mercy, or benefit.",
      "active_motifs": "affectionate gift-giving `ه د ي:B004/m01`; conferred benefit `ن ع م:B001/m02`; mercy enacted as benefaction `ر ح م:B001/m02`; transfer into another's ownership `م ل ك:B002/m02`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` (`م ل ك`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)",
      "synthesis": "Affection supplies the social motive, the gift supplies the transferred object, ownership change completes the act, and benefaction names its intended effect on the recipient.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:1",
        "1:3",
        "1:4",
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Property, Measure, and Legitimate Transfer",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Goods and obligations move between parties through ownership, valuation, replacement, compensation, gift, or offering.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "Financial debt and equal coinage join a livestock market and its stray property, while compensation and substitution offer a regulated alternative to uncompensated loss.",
      "key": "D",
      "name": "Consecrated Offering",
      "reading_type": "mixed",
      "scene_or_process": "Livestock or goods are selected from owned wealth and directed toward the sanctuary as an act of worship.",
      "active_motifs": "consecrated offering to the sanctuary `ه د ي:B005/m01`; livestock wealth `ن ع م:B005/m01`; the worshipped deity `ء ل ه:B001/m02`; worshipful submission `ع ب د:B003/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:1 `ٱللَّهِ` and 1:2 `لِلَّهِ` (`ء ل ه`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)",
      "synthesis": "Livestock supplies the transferable material, consecration fixes its destination, and worship supplies the relation that distinguishes an offering from an ordinary gift.",
      "ayah_refs": [

        "1:5",
        "1:6",
        "1:7"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Household Continuity and Protected Belonging",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Kinship is produced and maintained through gestation, nurture, marriage, covenant, and defense of household boundaries.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The mercy root opens into womb and blood relation, while guidance becomes both bride-conveyance and the protected status of a refugee under covenant.",
      "key": "A",
      "name": "Kinship, Fosterage, and Continuing Care",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "A child belongs to a network of blood and acquired kin whose caregiver assumes responsibility for nurture and continuity.",
      "active_motifs": "close kinship `ر ح م:B002/m01`; maintaining kin ties `ر ح م:B002/m02`; stepchild `ر ب ب:B005/m01`; foster caregiver `ر ب ب:B005/m02`; progressive child-rearing `ر ب ب:B002/m02`; guardianship `ق و م:B004/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`)",
      "synthesis": "Blood relation supplies one form of belonging, fosterage supplies another, and guardianship turns both into continuing obligations. Progressive nurture is the operation that converts formal relation into lived care.",
      "ayah_refs": [
        "1:1",
        "1:3",
        "1:2",
        "1:6"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Household Continuity and Protected Belonging",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Kinship is produced and maintained through gestation, nurture, marriage, covenant, and defense of household boundaries.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The mercy root opens into womb and blood relation, while guidance becomes both bride-conveyance and the protected status of a refugee under covenant.",
      "key": "C",
      "name": "Marriage and Bride-Conveyance",
      "reading_type": "latent/lexical",
      "scene_or_process": "A marriage contract establishes a new household relation and the bride is formally conveyed to her spouse, accompanied by affectionate transfer.",
      "active_motifs": "marriage contract `م ل ك:B004/m01`; bride conveyed to her husband `ه د ي:B006/m01`; kinship relation `ر ح م:B002/m01`; affectionate gift `ه د ي:B004/m01`",
      "ayah_anchors": "1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` (`م ل ك`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`)",
      "synthesis": "Contract creates the relation, conveyance enacts the transition between households, kinship names the resulting bond, and the gift supplies a reciprocal social gesture around the transfer.",
      "ayah_refs": [

        "1:3",
        "1:4",
        "1:6"
      ]
    },
    {
      "parent_channel": "Household Continuity and Protected Belonging",
      "parent_semantic_invariant": "Kinship is produced and maintained through gestation, nurture, marriage, covenant, and defense of household boundaries.",
      "parent_surprising_reach": "The mercy root opens into womb and blood relation, while guidance becomes both bride-conveyance and the protected status of a refugee under covenant.",
      "key": "D",

      "synthesis": "Naming identifies the protected person, covenant establishes the relation, certification makes it publicly actionable, and indemnity defines the consequence of violating that protection.",

            "neighbor_coverage_note": "Bütün adaylar değerlendirildi; doğru yolu bulma alanındaki en yakın örtüşme ve açık karşıtlık sınırı en iyi belirlediği için iki ilişki yayımlandı.",
            "branch_image_ar": "دلالة بلطف إلى الطريق والحق",
            "what_is_ar": "يدخل فيه الهدى والهداية بمعنى الرشاد والدلالة والبيان وتعريف الطريق أو الحق والدين والاهتداء والتوفيق الإلهي وقبول الهدى",
            "what_is_not_ar": "لا يدخل فيه الهدية بمعنى العطاء ولا الهدي المهدى إلى الحرم ولا مجرد أول الشيء إلا إذا كان للتقدم والإرشاد",
            "source_phrase_ar": "الهدى نقيض الضلالة؛ هدي فاهتدى (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الهدى الرشاد والدلالة؛ هداه الله للدين هدى؛ أولم يبين لهم؛ هديته الطريق والبيت هداية أي عرفته (sihah)؛ الهدى البيان وإخراج شيء إلى شيء والطاعة والورع؛ دله على الطريق (tahdhib)؛ الهداية دلالة بلطف؛ تعريف الطرق؛ التوفيق (mufradat)؛ التقدم للإرشاد؛ هديته الطريق هداية؛ الهدى خلاف الضلالة (maqayis)",
            "sources": [
              "AY",
              "SI",
              "TA",
              "MU",
              "MQ"
            ],
            "source_note": {}
          },
          {
            "branch_ref": "root_001583/B002",
            "identity_judgment": {
              "status": "accepted",
              "rationale": "Kaynak sözü bir işin yönünü ve amacını, kişinin gidişini, görünür tutumunu ve yöntemini, ayrıca sürmekte olan söz veya işi bırakmamayı birlikte verir. Verilen çerçeve bu çok parçalı fakat bağlantılı kullanımı korur.",
              "boundary_note": "Dal yalnızca genel bir yaşam tarzı değildir; yön ve amaç bildiren kullanımlarla belirli bir işte veya söylemde kalma kullanımını da içerir."
            },
            "lexicalization_scope": {
              "branch_kind": "mixed_non_bare",
              "note": "Yön, gidiş ve tutum bildiren biçimler ile kalıplaşmış benzerlik ve yineleme kullanımları ayrı yüzler olarak tutulur; özel kullanımlar çıplak kökün tümüne yayılmaz."
            },

            "neighbor_coverage_note": "Tüm yön, yöntem, alışkanlık ve benzerlik adayları değerlendirildi; kapsam ayrımını en açık gösteren tek yakın anlamlı ilişki seçildi.",
            "branch_image_ar": "جهة الأمر وسيرته وقصده",
            "what_is_ar": "يدخل فيه هدي الأمر أو هديته بمعنى جهته ووجهته وقصده وسيرته وطريقته وسمته وما يلازم ذلك من لزوم الحديث أو العمل والمماثلة في هدياها",
            "what_is_not_ar": "لا يدخل فيه الهدى بمعنى الإرشاد الديني وحده ولا الهدية العطية ولا مشي التمايل المعتمد على اثنين",
            "source_phrase_ar": "خذ في هديتك أي فيما كنت فيه من الحديث أو العمل ولا تعدل عنه؛ هدية أمره وسيرته؛ هدى هدي فلان أي سار سيرته (sihah)؛ هدية أمره أي جهة أمره؛ هديت به أي قصدت به؛ هديه أي سمته؛ ليس لهذا الأمر هدية ولا قبلة ولا دبرة ولا وجهة؛ هدياها أي مثلها أو أعاودك (tahdhib)؛ هدية فلان وهديه أي طريقته (mufradat)؛ نظر فلان هدي أمره أي جهته؛ ما أحسن هديته أي هديه؛ رميت بآخر هدياه أي قصده (maqayis)",

            ],

            "what_is_ar": "يدخل فيه الهادي أو الهادية بمعنى أول الشيء وما تقدم منه كأعناق الخيل وأول رعيلها وأوائل الوحش ورقبة الشاة ونصل السهم والعصا أو الدليل حين يتقدمان",
            "what_is_not_ar": "لا يدخل فيه الهدى المجرد عن معنى التقدم ولا الهدية العطية ولا الهادي الراكس في البيدر",
            "source_phrase_ar": "الهادي من كل شيء أوله؛ هوادي الخيل أعناقها أو أول رعيل؛ العصا هاديا لأنها تتقدمه؛ الدليل يسمى هاديا لتقدمه (ayn)؛ هادي السهم نصله؛ الهادي العنق؛ هوادي الخيل أعناقها أو أول رعيل؛ الهاديات أوائل الوحش (sihah)؛ الهادية من كل شيء أوله وما تقدم منه؛ هادية الشاة الرقبة؛ هوادي الخيل أعناقها أو أول رعيل؛ هاديات الوحش أوائلها (tahdhib)؛ هوادي الوحش متقدماتها الهادية لغيرها (mufradat)؛ كل متقدم لذلك هاد؛ هوادي الخيل أعناقها؛ هاديها أول رعيل؛ الهادية العصا لأنها تتقدم ممسكها (maqayis)",

            "branch_image_ar": "بعثة لطف وهدية إلى ذي مودة",
            "what_is_ar": "يدخل فيه الهدية والهدايا والإهداء بمعنى العطية اللطيفة إلى ذي مودة والتهادي بين الناس والمهدى الطبق والمهداء كثير الإهداء",

            "source_phrase_ar": "الهدية ما أهديت إلى ذي مودة من بر (ayn)؛ الهدية واحدة الهدايا؛ أهديت له وإليه؛ المهدى ما يهدى فيه؛ التهادي أن يهدي بعضهم إلى بعض؛ المهداء الذي من عادته أن يهدي (sihah)؛ أهديت الهدية إهداء؛ امرأة مهداء؛ المهدى الطبق الذي يهدى عليه (tahdhib)؛ الهدية مختصة باللطف؛ المهدى الطبق؛ المهداء من يكثر إهداء الهدية (mufradat)؛ الهدية ما أهديت من لطف إلى ذي مودة؛ المهدي الطبق تهدى عليه (maqayis)",

            "neighbor_coverage_note": "Eş, evlilik, düğün, gelin taşıma ve evliliksizlik adayları değerlendirildi; aynı olay aşamasını paylaşan en yakın komşu seçildi.",
            "branch_image_ar": "العروس المهدية إلى زوجها",
            "what_is_ar": "يدخل فيه هديت المرأة أو العروس إلى زوجها وهداؤها وكونها مهدية أو هدي في معنى مفعول",
            "what_is_not_ar": "لا يدخل فيه الهدية العادية ولا الهدي إلى الحرم إلا من جهة أصل الإرسال",
            "source_phrase_ar": "الهداء مصدر قولك هديت المرأة إلى زوجها؛ وهي مهدية وهدي (sihah)؛ هديت العروس فأنا أهديها هداء؛ أهدى الرجل امرأته جمعها إليه وضمها؛ المرأة سميت هديا لأنها كالأسيرة عند زوجها أو لأنها تهدى إلى زوجها (tahdhib)؛ الهدي يقال في العروس؛ هديت العروس إلى زوجها (mufradat)؛ الهدى العروس وقد هديت إلى بعلها هداء (maqayis)",

              "rationale": "Kaynak sözü sakinlik ile bozgun halinde kaçan kişinin telaşlı hızından uzak, düzgün ve güzel bir yürüyüş ya da tutumu birlikte verir. Dal çerçevesi sakinliği tek başına bırakmayıp görünüş ve davranış niteliğiyle bağlar.",
              "boundary_note": "Bu dal salt hareketsizlik değildir; telaşsız ilerleme ve düzgün görünür tutum birlikte bulunur."
            },
            "lexicalization_scope": {
              "branch_kind": "bare",
              "note": "Çıplak sakin ve düzgün tutum anlamı tanımlanır; başka kökteki durulma eylemi veya genel yaşam tarzı anlamları eklenmez."
            },
            "concept_map": {
              "definition": "Bozguna uğramış birinin telaşlı kaçışına benzemeyen sakin, ölçülü ve düzgün ilerleyiş ya da görünür tutumdur.",
              "facets": [
                {
                  "facet_id": "F001",
                  "role": "core",
                  "statement": "Hareket ve tutumda sakinlik ve telaşsızlık bildirir.",
                  "claim_ids": [
                    "bc_001"
                  ]
                },
                {
                  "facet_id": "F002",
                  "role": "core",
                  "statement": "Sakinliğe düzgün ve güzel bir görünür tutum eşlik eder.",
                  "claim_ids": [
                    "bc_001"
                  ]
                },
                {
                  "facet_id": "F003",
                  "role": "example",
                  "statement": "Bozguna uğramış kişinin acele kaçışı, bu sakin ilerleyişin karşı örneğidir.",
                  "claim_ids": [
                    "bc_001"
                  ]
                }
              ]
            },
            "source_synthesis": {
              "common_summary": "Kaynaklar sakinlik ve güzel tutumu birlikte verir; bozgun halinde kaçan kişinin aceleci yürüyüşünün reddi, bu telaşsız ve düzgün ilerleyişi belirginleştirir.",
              "common_claim_ids": [

              ],

            },
            "concept_gloss": {

            "neighbor_distinctions": [
              {
                "neighbor_ref": "root_000608/B001",
                "relation_type": "near_synonym",
                "boundary_match": "partial",
                "focus_only": "Bozgun kaçışının tersine ölçülü ilerleme ve güzel görünür tutum bu dalın belirgin sınırıdır.",
                "neighbor_only": "Denizin durgunluğu ile yaşam veya eylemin sıkıntısız, sertliksiz oluşu komşu dalın daha geniş kapsamındadır.",
                "gloss": "sakin ve telaşsız tutum",
                "shared_zone": "İki dal da acele, sertlik ve kaygıdan uzak sakin bir hareket ya da davranışı anlatır.",
                "distinction": "Bu dal sakinliği kişinin düzgün ilerleyişi ve görünür tutumuyla sınırlar; komşu dal sakinliği doğaya, yaşama ve türlü eylemlere yayar."
              }
            ],
            "neighbor_coverage_note": "Sakinlik, yumuşaklık, ağırbaşlılık, rahatlık ve güzel görünüş adayları değerlendirildi; hareket ve tutum sınırını en iyi gösteren komşu seçildi.",
            "branch_image_ar": "هدي السكون وحسن الهيئة",
            "what_is_ar": "يدخل فيه الهدي بمعنى السكون وترك إسراع المنهزم مع حسن الهدي أو الهيئة",
            "what_is_not_ar": "لا يدخل فيه المهموز هدأ إذا أريد باب السكون في أصل ه د ء ولا السيرة العامة إلا إذا نص المصدر على السكون",
            "source_phrase_ar": "الهدي السكون؛ ما هدى هدي مهزوم؛ لم يسرع إسراع المنهزم ولكن على سكون وهدي حسن (ayn)؛ الهدي السكون؛ لم يسرع إسراع المنهزم ولكن على سكون وحسن هدي (tahdhib)",
            "sources": [
              "AY",
              "TA"
            ],
            "source_note": {}
          },
          {
            "branch_ref": "root_001583/B011",
            "identity_judgment": {
              "status": "accepted",
              "rationale": "Kaynak sözü bir kişiye övgü veya yergi şiiri sunmayı ve şiir yoluyla karşılıklı yergileşmeyi açıkça ayırır. Verilen dal çerçevesi tek yönlü şiir sunma ile karşılıklı şiirsel atışma arasındaki katılımcı farkını korur.",
              "boundary_note": "Dal yalnızca şiir gönderme ve şiirle karşılıklı yergileşme yapılarıyla sınırlıdır; maddi armağan veya genel sözlü tartışma değildir."
            },
            "lexicalization_scope": {
              "branch_kind": "non_bare",
              "note": "Anlam yalnızca belirtilen şiir sunma ve karşılıklı şiirsel yergileşme birimlerine bağlıdır; çıplak kök için genel bir anlam sayılmaz."
            },
            "concept_map": {
              "definition": "Bir kişiye övgü veya yergi içeren bir şiir sunmak ya da iki kişinin şiirle karşılıklı olarak birbirini yermesidir.",

            "source_synthesis": {

                "neighbor_only": "Genel konuşmayı, övgüyü veya yönelişi herhangi bir kişiye ya da yöne çevirmek komşu dalın kapsamındadır.",

            "neighbor_coverage_note": "Şiir türü, uyak, söz yöneltme, karşılıklılık, yergi ve çok konuşma adayları değerlendirildi; şiire bağlı yöneltme sınırını en iyi açıklayan ilişki seçildi.",
            "branch_image_ar": "إهداء الشعر ومهاداته",
            "what_is_ar": "يدخل فيه إهداء المديح أو الهجاء شعرا إلى إنسان ومهاداته الشعر بمعنى مهاجاته",
            "what_is_not_ar": "لا يدخل فيه الهدية المادية ولا مطلق المهاجاة إذا لم ترد بلفظ هادى أو أهدى",
            "source_phrase_ar": "الإهداء أن تهدي إلى إنسان مديحا أو هجاء شعرا (ayn)؛ هاداني فلان الشعر وهاديته أي هاجاني وهاجيته (tahdhib)",

        "root_profile": {
          "summary": "Bu kökün dalları doğru yolu gösterme ve izlenen yön çekirdeğinden önde bulunmaya; incelik armağanına, kutsal sunuya, gelinin götürülmesine ve korunan kişiye; ayrıca sallantılı yürüyüş, kişi niteliği, sakin tutum ve şiir sunma gibi ayrışmış alanlara yayılır. Anlam örgüsü hem göndermek ve öne yöneltmek çevresinde gelişen bağları hem de tarihsel olarak ayrılaşmış adlandırmaları birlikte taşır.",
          "polysemy": "polysemic",
          "organization": "mixed"
        },

            "identity_judgment": {
              "status": "qualified",
              "rationale": "Kaynak ifadesi, sözcüğün temel olarak kadınlar dışındaki erkekler topluluğunu anlattığını, kadınların ancak ikincil biçimde kapsama girebildiğini ve insan dışı varlıklara aktarılabildiğini gösterir. Bu nedenle genel ve cinsiyetçe sınırsız bir topluluk adı olarak sunulmamalıdır.",
              "boundary_note": "Temel kapsam erkekler topluluğudur; kadınların katılımı ve insan dışına aktarım ikincil kullanımlardır."
            },
            "lexicalization_scope": {
              "branch_kind": "mixed_non_bare",
              "note": "Tanım, temel topluluk anlamını bağlı anlatımlardaki yandaş ve soy çevresi anlamından ayırır."
            },
            "concept_map": {
              "definition": "Temelde erkeklerden oluşan insan topluluğudur; bir erkeğin yandaş ve yakın soy çevresini de anlatabilir. Kadınların topluluğa bağlı olarak kapsanması ve sözün insan dışı varlıklara aktarılması ikincildir.",
              "facets": [
                {
                  "facet_id": "F001",
                  "role": "core",
                  "statement": "Temel gönderge, kadınlardan ayrı düşünülen erkekler topluluğudur.",
                  "claim_ids": [
                    "bc_001"
                  ]
                },
                {
                  "facet_id": "F002",
                  "role": "extension",
                  "statement": "Bir erkeğin yandaşları ile yakın soy çevresi, kadınların bağlı olarak kapsanması ve insan dışına aktarım bu çekirdeğe bağlıdır.",
                  "claim_ids": [
                    "bc_001"
                  ]
                }
              ]

            "branch_image_ar": "قوام وعماد ومعاش",
            "what_is_ar": "يدخل فيه القيام أو القوام لما يقوم به الشيء ويثبت، وعماد الأمر ونظامه وملاكه، وما يقيم العيش والجسم والمعاش",

            "source_phrase_ar": "هذا الأمر لا قومية له أي لا قوام له؛ القوام من العيش ما يقيمك ويغنيك؛ القيام العماد؛ قوام كل شيء ما استقام به (ayn)؛ قوام الأمر نظامه وعماده؛ قوام الأمر ملاكه؛ جعل الله لكم قياما (sihah)؛ قوام الأمر وملاكه؛ تقيمكم فتقومون بها؛ قوام الجسم تمامه؛ قوام كل شيء ما استقام به (tahdhib)؛ القيام والقوام اسم لما يقوم به الشيء؛ جعلها مما يمسككم؛ قياما للناس أي قواما لهم يقوم به معاشهم ومعادهم (mufradat)؛ قوام الدين والحق أي به يقوم (maqayis)",

            "source_phrase_ar": "القامة مقدار قيام الرجل كهيئة الرجل يبنى على شفير بئر؛ قائم السيف مقبضه؛ قائمة السرير والخوان والدابة (ayn)؛ القامة البكرة بأداتها؛ قائم السيف وقائمته مقبضه؛ القائمة واحدة قوائم الدواب؛ المقوم الخشبة التي يمسكها الحراث (sihah)؛ القامة البكرة التي يستقى بها الماء؛ النعامة الخشبة المعترضة ثم تعلق القامة؛ قائم السيف مقبضه وما سوى ذلك فهو قائمة (tahdhib)؛ القامة البكرة بأداتها (maqayis)",
            "sources": [
              "AY",
              "SI",
              "TA",
              "MQ"
            ],
            "source_note": {}
          },

        "root_profile": {
          "summary": "Anlam örgüsü, dik durma ve bir şeyi ayakta tutma düşüncesinden topluluk, kararlı girişim, gözetim, sürdürme, yerleşme, yerine geçme, düzgünlük, dayanak, değer, boy ve araç parçası gibi çok sayıda alana yayılır. Son gün, mücadele, ölçün ağırlık, durgunluk, öğle ortası, pazar canlılığı, ağrı, hayvan hastalığı ve özel görme kaybı dalları ise belirli biçim ve yapılarda yerleşmiş ayrı anlam kümeleri oluşturur.",
          "polysemy": "polysemic",
          "organization": "mixed"
        },

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response, staged
  reveal sequence, `changed_reading`, or confidence movement;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by related root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

Start each paragraph with reader meaning before grammar. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi
Allah'a verir; bunu fiille değil, sabit bir ad cümlesiyle yapar" over "Bu âyet,
tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
confirm in friction that there was no live misconception, replacement risk, or
counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
